PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-284 / DO-672. Hosted GlucoRx Sign In is reachable. The protocol Test Data table lists every supported
application language for the build under test. Record that language list before execution.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Open the hosted GlucoRx Sign In page and note the
current selection in the language selector.
2. For each supported language listed in Test Data,
select that language.
3. In each language, inspect the “Sign in to your
account” heading, Email field, Password field, Forgot
Password action, Sign In button and Register link.
4. With the credential fields empty, tap Sign In once
and observe the required-field validation, then return
to the language selector and repeat for the next
language.
1. Every supported language listed in Test Data can be
selected on the logged-out Sign In flow.
2. The sign-in heading, field labels, actions and required-
field validation are presented using the selected language
resources.
3. Language availability on the logged-out Sign In flow is
not removed solely because of the device/account region,
and the page remains usable in each tested language.

VP-APP-HOSTED-REG-018   Select and lock the account region during registration   FAIL
SECTION 7 · Hosted Registration
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-281 · DO-669 · DO-668 · DO-670 · DO-671
OBSERVED No country/region selection available on the registration
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-281 / DO-669. Protocol Test Data identifies a registration region/country available to the test build and a
fresh test email. All other required registration data are available.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Open the hosted registration flow and locate the
region/country selection used to establish the account
region.
2. Before submitting, inspect the user-facing information
associated with region selection and record whether it
states that the selection determines where health data are
stored and cannot be changed later.
3. Select the Test Data region/country and complete
registration, email activation and sign in through the
normal user flow.
4. Open Settings > User Profile and inspect the
Country/region field and any available edit control.
1. Registration provides the controlled account-region
selection and informs the user before completion that
the selection determines health-data storage location
and cannot be changed later.
2. The selected region/country is retained for the
created account.
3. After registration, the user cannot change the
account region through an in-app operation.
4. If the required region selection or required notice is
absent from the tested registration UI, record the case
as FAIL rather than substituting Profile Setup
behavior.
NO FM
VP-APP-HOSTED-REG-019   Set and lock glucose unit from registration region   FAIL
SECTION 7 · Hosted Registration
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-282 · DO-670 · DO-668 · DO-669 · DO-671
OBSERVED Ambiguous, can not select region and changing device/phone/OS language doesn't reflect on the app neither app
doesn't seem to be aware of device/phone/OS language change
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-282 / DO-670. Protocol Test Data identifies at least one supported registration region and its required
glucose unit. If the approved region matrix contains a region that permits multiple units, Test Data also identifies that region and the permitted
choices. Fresh registration data are available for each required scenario.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. In a fresh registration, select the Test Data region that maps to a
single glucose unit and observe the glucose-unit behavior before
submitting.
2. Complete registration/activation/sign-in and reach Tell us about you
/ Profile Setup; record Country and the displayed Glucose unit.
3. If Test Data identifies a supported multi-unit region, repeat with a
second fresh account: select that region, choose the permitted unit
during registration, complete the explicit confirmation required by the
flow, then sign in and record the applied unit.
4. On the post-login Profile Setup screen, confirm the account unit
cannot be changed independently to create a Country/Glucose Unit
mismatch.
1. For a single-unit region, the correct
glucose unit is applied without an
independent user choice at registration.
2. For a supported multi-unit region, the
user can select only a permitted unit and
must complete the controlled
confirmation before proceeding.
3. After login, the account-level unit
matches the registered region/selection
and is applied to the Profile Setup
glucose-unit state.
4. The user cannot independently change
the unit post-registration to create an
inconsistent Country/Glucose Unit
combination.
NO FM

VP-APP-HOSTED-REG-020   Unsupported registration language warning and recovery   FAIL
SECTION 7 · Hosted Registration
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-283 · DO-671 · DO-668 · DO-669 · DO-670
OBSERVED Ambiguous, can not select region and changing device/phone/OS language doesn't reflect on the app neither app
seems to be aware of device/phone/OS language change
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-283 / DO-671. Protocol Test Data identifies a registration region, a phone/display language that is not
supported for authenticated content in that region, and the region’s approved fallback language. The phone language can be changed using
normal OS controls.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Set the phone/display language to the
unsupported-for-region language recorded in
Test Data.
2. Open hosted registration and select the target
Test Data region.
3. Observe the warning shown before
registration proceeds.
4. Use the warning option that allows the user to
stop/correct the selection, then return and
correct the region/language as needed.
5. Repeat the mismatch and choose the warning
option that allows registration to continue,
where provided by the controlled flow.
1. A non-blocking warning is presented when the registration
language is not supported by the selected account region.
2. The warning tells the user that the subsequent logged-in
application content will not be available in the currently
displayed language and allows the user to return/correct the
region or language.
3. The phone-language mismatch alone does not make account
registration impossible; the user can continue if they deliberately
accept the controlled warning path.
4. If the controlled warning is not present, record FAIL rather
than using a generic browser-language message as a substitute.
NO FM

OBSERVED App still shows apple health access as granted after disabling the access from Health app

# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS

Profile > Privacy > Apps > CGMx 1; Android:

the affected platform-health data.

revoked platform-health data as currently authorized.
Glucose/CGM functionality remains available and the user
can re-authorize health integration through the supported
permission path.

PRECONDITIONS Req: DO-877. Disposable verification account; current and replacement inboxes available; internet available.

Security > Change Email.

SECTION 50 · Logout
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-233 · DO-808 · DO-809
OBSERVED Doesn't mention local CGM disconnection/reconnection after login and paused pending uploads. Mentions that
glucose monitoring and alarms would stop
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-233 / DO-808. A controlled local or remote monitoring session is active and pending-upload implications
can be observed where applicable.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Open Settings > Account Security >
Logout while the session is active.
2. Read the confirmation before
proceeding.
3. Verify the warning addresses alarm
unavailability, local CGM
disconnection/reconnection after login,
and paused pending uploads as applicable.
4. Confirm Logout.
5. Observe the logged-out state.
6. Re-login to the same account and
observe recovery of the expected session.
Before logout from an active monitoring session, the user is warned
that local and remotely monitored alarm functionality will be
unavailable, the local CGM will be disconnected and must reconnect
after login, and pending uploads are paused. After logout protected
monitoring content is unavailable. After same-account re-login,
retained expected-session state can recover/reconnect according to the
approved design.

VP-APP-LOGOUT-007   Temporary network failure must not falsely complete logout   FAIL
SECTION 50 · Logout
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-233 · DO-808 · DO-809
OBSERVED App logs out when internet is not connected
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-233 / DO-808. User is authenticated. Internet is initially available, then disabled using normal OS controls
to exercise a temporary connectivity failure.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Confirm normal authenticated
state.
2. Disable Wi-Fi/mobile data.
3. Attempt Logout and observe
the result.
4. Verify the app does not falsely
report a successful logout for the
temporary connectivity failure.
5. Restore connectivity.
6. Retry Logout.

falsely complete and the user is informed. After connectivity is restored, logout
can be retried and completed. The case does not generalize this behavior to
every possible backend error classification.

VP-APP-WELCOME-NAV-004   Background/resume and root navigation recovery   PASS
SECTION 3 · Welcome Navigation
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY Batch 3 · Welcome Navigation
PRECONDITIONS Welcome screen is displayed and the user is logged out.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. From the Welcome screen, place CGMx 1 in
the background.
2. Return CGMx 1 to the foreground and
observe the displayed screen.
3. Android only: while Welcome is the root app
screen, press the system Back control once.
4. Reopen CGMx 1 and observe the
unauthenticated screen.
1. Foregrounding the app returns to the Welcome screen without
starting registration, authentication or another account action.
2. On Android, system Back from the root Welcome screen does
not open an earlier app-owned screen or start an account action.
3. Reopening CGMx 1 returns to a usable unauthenticated
Welcome state without duplicate navigation or crash.
YES FM

duplicate screen or corrupted navigation state.

VP-APP-SIGNIN-005   Hosted sign-in with no internet and recovery   PASS
SECTION 4 · Native Sign In Tab
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY Batch 4 · Native Sign In Tab
PRECONDITIONS Native Sign In tab is open. First confirm normal internet access, then disable Wi-Fi and mobile data using the device
controls.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. With internet disabled, tap Sign in
with GlucoRx SSO.
2. Observe the browser/authentication
surface.
3. Return to the Native Sign In screen
without authenticating.
4. Restore internet connectivity.
5. Tap Sign in with GlucoRx SSO again
and observe the hosted page.
1. While internet is unavailable, the hosted authentication flow does not
authenticate the user and a visible network-unavailable/browser failure
state is presented.
2. Returning from that failure leaves the app on a usable unauthenticated
Sign In state.
3. After internet is restored, retry opens the hosted GlucoRx “Sign in to
your account” page once, with its Email and Password controls available.
YES FM

VP-APP-REGISTER-CONSENT-003   Mandatory consent gating   PASS
SECTION 5 · Native Create Account and Consent
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DO-805 · DI-785
PRECONDITIONS Create Account tab is open; Terms unchecked; Privacy unchecked. Direct requirement: DO-805 / DI-785.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Observe Register with GlucoRx with both consent
controls unchecked.
2. Select only the Terms consent.
3. Select the Privacy consent as well.
4. Deselect either required consent.
1. Register with GlucoRx is disabled while both required
consents are unchecked.
2. Register remains disabled while only one required
consent is selected.
3. Register becomes enabled when all required consent is
selected.
4. Deselecting either required consent disables Register
again.
YES FM

EXPECTED
INITIALS

displayed.

notification is delivered.

restore notification permission in the
native prompt/OS Settings.
6. Return to CGMx 1 and confirm the
warning clears.
7. Produce another controlled
High/Low reading and verify
notification delivery.

EXPECTED
INITIALS

Setup / transmitter pairing screen.

application.

5. Observe the first screen/flow presented after

6. Attempt to access normal clinical functionality
without completing the previously incomplete
onboarding.

application checks that account's onboarding/configuration
state.
5. Because initial setup was not fully completed, the
setup/onboarding flow is re-presented after login instead of
being silently treated as complete.

the application follows the defined Skip behavior and may
navigate to Home; this does not invalidate the required re-
presentation behavior.
YES FM

triggering reading and no duplicate alert is generated for the same event.

VP-APP-LOW-015   Historical RACP/backfill Low readings do not trigger new Low notifications   PASS
SECTION 16 · Low Glucose Alert
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-175 · DO-867
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-175 / DO-867. Active CGM session, notification permission enabled, and controlled conditions are
available to create missing historical readings that include at least one Low threshold breach.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Record the configured Low threshold
and last live reading/index before
interruption.
2. Create a sustained Bluetooth
disconnection using the approved real-
device method so measurements become
missing.
3. Allow the transmitter to record at least
one historical Low reading during the
interruption.
4. Restore Bluetooth and allow missing
readings to backfill/RACP.
5. Verify the historical Low reading
appears in history/Journal.
6. Observe the system notification
surface during backfill.
Recovered historical Low threshold-breach readings are added to
history/Journal but do not generate a new Low system notification
merely because they arrive during RACP/backfill. New notifications
remain limited to eligible live measurements.
YES FM
EVIDENCE

SECTION 16 · Low Glucose Alert
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-858 · DO-946
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-858 / DO-946. Critical-low alarm can be generated using approved test data. The available snooze controls
are enabled. Approved procedure permits app/phone restart during the test.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Trigger the critical-low
alarm and snooze it.
2. Attempt repeated snoozes
through the controlled overall
maximum window.
3. Toggle applicable snooze
settings off/on where available.
4. Restart the app/phone during
the snooze period and repeat
the attempt.
Critical-low snooze cannot be extended beyond the requirement maximum by
repeated snoozing, toggling settings, or app/phone restart. Snooze state is retained
sufficiently to enforce the overall limit using trusted time where available.

final status, glucose value and update time.

presented as fresh/LIVE and the user receives
understandable recovery guidance.

automatic reconnection and returns to LIVE only after
fresh sensor data is received.

SECTION 32 · Time in Range Detail
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DO-914
PRECONDITIONS Req: DO-914. Historical glucose exists for a selectable period and one permitted Low or High threshold change can
be made through Settings.

EXPECTED
INITIALS

record the selected period plus all

change record.
7. Restore the original threshold after
evidence capture.

statistics are recalculated for the same data window. If the available
history contains no readings whose classification changes, record the
test-data dependency rather than manipulating data.

SECTION 37 · Meal / Carbohydrate Event Entry
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-198 · DO-781
OBSERVED Meal type is Time taken field on the app
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-198 / DO-781. Carbohydrate-entry form is open.

EXPECTED
INITIALS

meal type.

and date/time, then tap Save.

one matching event is present.
The carbohydrate event is saved once and Journal shows the
corresponding grams, meal type and date/time.
YES FM

VP-APP-MEAL-005   Validate carbohydrate amount in grams   PASS
SECTION 37 · Meal / Carbohydrate Event Entry
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-199 · DO-782 · DI-30 · DO-676
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-199 / DO-782; DI-30 / DO-676. Carbohydrate-entry form is open.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Enter 1 g with otherwise valid
fields and confirm it can be saved.
2. Enter 200 g in a new entry and
confirm it can be saved.
3. Attempt 0, a negative value if
permitted, 201, 1.5 if permitted,
and non-numeric text if permitted.
4. Attempt to save each invalid
input.
Only whole-number carbohydrate values from 1 g through 200 g inclusive
are accepted. Zero, negative, above-200, fractional and non-numeric values
are not saved as valid carbohydrate events, with user-visible validation where
applicable.
YES FM
EVIDENCE

VP-APP-MEAL-006   Display saved carbohydrate events in the Carbohydrates chart   PASS
SECTION 37 · Meal / Carbohydrate Event Entry
METHOD / PRIORITY Manual - Real Device
TRACEABILITY DI-200 · DO-783 · DI-199 · DO-782
PRECONDITIONS Req: DI-200 / DO-783; DI-199 / DO-782. At least one manually entered meal exists in the selected period; use Test
Data with known meal type and grams.
# STEP EXPECTED AS
EXPECTED
INITIALS
1 1. Save or identify a manual meal event
and record its meal type, carbohydrate
grams and date/time.
2. On Home, locate the Meals chart and
confirm the latest grams/time correspond
to the event.
3. Open the Carbohydrates detail.
4. Select a period containing the event.
5. Inspect the time-based bar chart, By
Meal Type section,
Total/Average/Peak/Tracked tiles and
Recent Entries.
The saved event is represented in the Carbohydrates chart in grams at
the corresponding date/time and contributes to its meal-type series. The
gram unit is used in the chart/statistics. Different meal types must be
visually distinguishable as required by DO-783; if the implementation
uses colour only without the required distinct patterns/legend, record
the mismatch.
YES FM

behavior is deterministic.

period/data coverage.
